Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the homomorphism theorem decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_128(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 128. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^295(S, F) is trivial. QED.
